00243: function hourBucketToSortValue(hour) {
00244:   const s = String(hour || "").trim().toLowerCase();
00245:   const m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{2}))?h?$/);
00246:   if (!m) return -1;
00247:   const hh = Number(m[1]);
00248:   const mm = Number(m[2] || 0);
00249:   if (!Number.isFinite(hh) || !Number.isFinite(mm)) return -1;
00250:   return hh * 60 + mm;
00251: }

00274: function clampColsFromItemMilharesCols(milharesCols, expectedCols = 4, perCol = 5) {
00275:   const colsArr = Array.isArray(milharesCols) ? milharesCols : [];
00276:   const out = [];
00277: 
00278:   for (let i = 0; i < Math.min(expectedCols, colsArr.length); i += 1) {
00279:     const dz = String(colsArr[i]?.dezena || "").trim();
00280:     const items0 = Array.isArray(colsArr[i]?.items) ? colsArr[i].items : [];
00281: 
00282:     const items = items0
00283:       .map(normalizeMilharStr)
00284:       .map((m) => (m && /^\d{4}$/.test(m) ? m : ""))
00285:       .slice(0, perCol);
00286:

00243: function hourBucketToSortValue(hour) {
00244:   const s = String(hour || "").trim().toLowerCase();
00245:   const m = s.match(/^(\d{1,2})(?::(\d{2}))?h?$/);
00246:   if (!m) return -1;
00247:   const hh = Number(m[1]);
00248:   const mm = Number(m[2] || 0);
00249:   if (!Number.isFinite(hh) || !Number.isFinite(mm)) return -1;
00250:   return hh * 60 + mm;
00251: }

00253: function wrapToDezena2(n) {
00254:   const x = Number(n);
00255:   if (!Number.isFinite(x)) return "";
00256:   const d = ((x % 100) + 100) % 100;
00257:   return String(d).padStart(2, "0");
00258: }

00260: function getDezenasFixasFromGrupo(grupo) {
00261:   const g = Number(grupo);
00262:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g < 1 || g > 25) return [];
00263: 
00264:   const start = (g - 1) * 4 + 1;
00265:   const out = [];

00268:     out.push(wrapToDezena2(start + i));
00269:   }
00270: 
00271:   return out;

01612:               key={`${slot?.targetYmd || "y"}_${slot?.targetHour || "h"}_${String(item?.grupo ?? i)}`}
01613:               item={item}
01614:               idx={i}
01615:               theme={t}
01616:               copiedAllKey={copiedAllKey}
01617:               copiedCellKey={copiedCellKey}
01618:               setCopiedAllKey={setCopiedAllKey}
01619:               setCopiedCellKey={setCopiedCellKey}
01620:               copyText={copyText}
01621:               build16={build16}
01622:               buildMilhares={buildMilhares}
01623:               build20={build20}
01624:             />
01625:           ))}
01626:         </div>
01627:       ) : (
01628:         <CompactTop3Result
01629:           slot={slot}
01630:           status={status}
01631:           statusLabel={statusLabel}
01632:           statusDetail={statusDetail}
01633:           analysis={analysis}
01634:         />
01635:       )}
01636:     </section>
01637:   );
01638: }

00172: 
00173: function formatGrupo(grupo) {
00174:   const g = Number(grupo);
00175:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g <= 0) return "—";
00176:   return String(Math.trunc(g)).padStart(2, "0");
00177: }
00178: 
00179: function normalizeMilharStr(v) {
00180:   const s = String(v || "").trim();
00181:   if (!s) return "";
00182:   const dig = s.replace(/\D+/g, "");
00183:   if (!dig) return "";
00184:   return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
00185: }

00197: function isYMD(s) {
00198:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());
00199: }
00200: 
00201: function ymdToBR(ymd) {
00202:   const s = String(ymd || "").trim();
00203:   if (!isYMD(s)) return s || "—";
00204:   const [y, m, d] = s.split("-");
00205:   return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;
00206: }
00207: 
00208: function addDaysYMDLocal(ymd, amount) {
00209:   const s = String(ymd || "").trim();
00210:   if (!isYMD(s)) return "";
00211: 
00212:   const [y, m, d] = s.split("-").map(Number);
00213:   const date = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
00214: 
00215:   date.setUTCDate(date.getUTCDate() + Number(amount || 0));
00216: 
00217:   return [
00218:     date.getUTCFullYear(),
00219:     String(date.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0"),
00220:     String(date.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0"),
00221:   ].join("-");
00222: }
00223: 
00224: function todayYMDLocalView() {
00225:   const now = new Date();
00226: 
00227:   return [
00228:     now.getFullYear(),
00229:     String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0"),
00230:     String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, "0"),
00231:   ].join("-");
00232: }

00234: function formatYmdHour(ymd, hour) {
00235:   const y = String(ymd || "").trim();
00236:   const h = String(hour || "").trim();
00237:   if (y && h) return `${ymdToBR(y)} ${h}`;
00238:   if (y) return ymdToBR(y);
00239:   if (h) return h;
00240:   return "—";
00241: }

00173: function formatGrupo(grupo) {

00175:   if (!Number.isFinite(g) || g <= 0) return "—";
00176:   return String(Math.trunc(g)).padStart(2, "0");

00179: function normalizeMilharStr(v) {
00180:   const s = String(v || "").trim();
00181:   if (!s) return "";
00182:   const dig = s.replace(/\D+/g, "");
00183:   if (!dig) return "";
00184:   return dig.length >= 4 ? dig.slice(-4) : dig.padStart(4, "0");
00185: }

00198:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());

00201: function ymdToBR(ymd) {
00202:   const s = String(ymd || "").trim();
00203:   if (!isYMD(s)) return s || "—";
00204:   const [y, m, d] = s.split("-");
00205:   return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;
00206: }

00208: function addDaysYMDLocal(ymd, amount) {
00209:   const s = String(ymd || "").trim();
00210:   if (!isYMD(s)) return "";
00211: 
00212:   const [y, m, d] = s.split("-").map(Number);
00213:   const date = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
00214: 
00215:   date.setUTCDate(date.getUTCDate() + Number(amount || 0));
00216: 
00217:   return [
00218:     date.getUTCFullYear(),
00219:     String(date.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0"),
00220:     String(date.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0"),
00221:   ].join("-");
00222: }

00198:   return /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(s || "").trim());
00199: }
00200: 
00201: function ymdToBR(ymd) {
00202:   const s = String(ymd || "").trim();
00203:   if (!isYMD(s)) return s || "—";
00204:   const [y, m, d] = s.split("-");
00205:   return `${d}/${m}/${y}`;
00206: }
00207: 
00208: function addDaysYMDLocal(ymd, amount) {
00209:   const s = String(ymd || "").trim();
00210:   if (!isYMD(s)) return "";
00211: 
00212:   const [y, m, d] = s.split("-").map(Number);
00213:   const date = new Date(Date.UTC(y, m - 1, d));
00214: 
00215:   date.setUTCDate(date.getUTCDate() + Number(amount || 0));
00216: 
00217:   return [
00218:     date.getUTCFullYear(),
00219:     String(date.getUTCMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0"),
00220:     String(date.getUTCDate()).padStart(2, "0"),
00221:   ].join("-");
00222: }

00224: function todayYMDLocalView() {
00225:   const now = new Date();
00226: 
00227:   return [
00228:     now.getFullYear(),
00229:     String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0"),
00230:     String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, "0"),
00231:   ].join("-");
00232: }
